# Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2015/1278 of 9 July 2015 amending Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014 laying down implementing technical standards with regard to supervisory reporting of institutions as regards instructions, templates and definitions (Text with EEA relevance)

> **Warning: changes not yet applied to this text (1).** Text as at 2023-07-11. See https://www.legislation.gov.uk/changes/affected/eur/2015/1278
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2015/1278

of 9 July 2015

amending Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014 laying down implementing technical standards with regard to supervisory reporting of institutions as regards instructions, templates and definitions

(Text with EEA relevance)

[^key-510aadb705b8be410da02ec4e4a4b501]

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION,

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union,

Having regard to Regulation (EU) No 575/2013 of 26 June 2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council on prudential requirements for credit institutions and investment firms and amending Regulation (EU) No 648/2012[^f00001] and in particular the fourth subparagraph of Article 99(5), the fourth subparagraph of Article 99(6), the third subparagraph of Article 101(4), the third subparagraph of Article 394(4), the fourth subparagraph of Article 415(3) and the third subparagraph of Article 430(2) thereof,

Whereas:

(1)

Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014[^f00002] specifies the requirements according to which institutions are required to report information relevant to their compliance with Regulation (EU) No 575/2013. Given that the regulatory framework established by Regulation (EU) No 575/2013 is gradually being supplemented and amended in its non-essential elements by the adoption of regulatory technical standards, then Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014 needs to be updated accordingly to reflect those rules; to provide further precision in the instructions and definitions used for the purposes of institutions' supervisory reporting.

(2)

In order to ensure a correct and uniform application of the requirements laid down in Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014, further precision should be provided to the templates, instructions and definitions used for the purposes of supervisory reporting. Therefore, for reasons of legal clarity, it is appropriate to replace several templates of Annexes I, III and IV and to amend some of the instructions laid down in Annexes II, V, IX and XVII.

(3)

To provide institutions and competent authorities with adequate time to implement the amendments set out in this Regulation, it should apply from 1 June 2015.

(4)

This Regulation is based on the draft implementing technical standards submitted by the European Banking Authority (EBA) to the Commission.

(5)

Given that the necessary amendments to Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014 do not involve significant changes in substantive terms, in accordance with the second subparagraph of Article 15(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1093/2010 of the European Parliament and of the Council[^f00003], the EBA has not conducted any open public consultation, considering that it would be disproportionate in relation to the scope and impact of the draft implementing technical standards concerned.

(6)

Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014 should be amended accordingly,

HAS ADOPTED THIS REGULATION:

**Article 1.** Implementing Regulation (EU) No 680/2014 is amended as follows:

- 1.. The templates numbered 1, 4, 6.2, 7, 8.1, 9.1, 9.2, 9.3, 17, 21 and 22 of Annex I are replaced by the respectively numbered templates set out in Annex I to this Regulation.
- 2.. Annex II is replaced by the text set out in Annex II to this Regulation.
- 3.. The templates numbered 1.3, 16, 20 and 46 of Annex III are replaced by the respectively numbered templates set out in Annex III to this Regulation.
- 4.. The templates numbered 1.3, 16, 20 and 46 of Annex IV are replaced by the respectively numbered templates set out in Annex IV to this Regulation.
- 5.. Annex V is replaced by the text set out in Annex V to this Regulation.
- 6.. Annex IX is replaced by the text set out in Annex VI to this Regulation.
- 7.. Annex XVII is replaced by the text set out in Annex VII to this Regulation.

**Article 2.** This Regulation shall enter into force on the twentieth day following that of its publication in the *Official Journal of the European Union*.

It shall apply from 1 June 2015.

This Regulation shall be binding in its entirety and directly applicable in all Member States.

Done at Brussels, 9 July 2015.

*For the Commission*

*The President*

Jean-Claude Juncker

## ANNEX II

> ANNEX II
>
> **REPORTING ON OWN FUNDS AND OWN FUNDS REQUIREMENTS**
>
> **PART I: GENERAL INSTRUCTIONS**
>
> 1\.
>
> STRUCTURE AND CONVENTIONS
>
> 1.1.
>
> STRUCTURE
>
> 1\.
>
> Overall, the framework consists of five blocks of templates:
>
> - ((a)) capital adequacy, an overview of regulatory capital; total risk exposure amount;
> - ((b)) group solvency, an overview of the fulfilment of the solvency requirements by all individual entities included in the scope of consolidation of the reporting entity
> - ((c)) credit risk (including counterparty, dilution and settlement risks);
> - ((d)) market risk (including position risk in trading book, foreign exchange risk, commodities risk and CVA risk);
> - ((e)) operational risk.
>
> 2\.
>
> For each template legal references are provided. Further detailed information regarding more general aspects of the reporting of each block of templates, instructions concerning specific positions as well as examples and validation rules are included in these Guidelines for implementation of the Common Reporting framework.
>
> 3\.
>
> Institutions report only those templates that are relevant depending on the approach used for determining own funds requirements.
>
> 1.2.
>
> NUMBERING CONVENTION
>
> 4\.
>
> The document follows the labelling convention set in the following table, when referring to the columns, rows and cells of the templates. These numerical codes are extensively used in the validation rules.
>
> 5\.
>
> The following general notation is followed in the instructions: {Template;Row;Column}.
>
> 6\.
>
> In the case of validations inside a template, in which only data points of that template is used, notations do not refer to a template: {Row;Column}.
>
> 7\.
>
> In the case of templates with only one column, only rows are referred to. {Template;Row}
>
> 8\.
>
> An asterisk sign is used to express that the validation is done for the rows or columns specified before.
>
> 1.3.
>
> SIGN CONVENTION
>
> 9\.
>
> Any amount that increases the own funds or the capital requirements shall be reported as a positive figure. On the contrary, any amount that reduces the total own funds or the capital requirements shall be reported as a negative figure. Where there is a negative sign (-) preceding the label of an item no positive figure is expected to be reported for that item.
>
> **PART II: TEMPLATE RELATED INSTRUCTIONS**
>
> 1\.
>
> CAPITAL ADEQUACY OVERVIEW (CA)
>
> 1.1.
>
> GENERAL REMARKS
>
> 10\.
>
> CA templates contain information about Pillar 1 numerators (own funds, Tier 1, Common Equity Tier 1), denominator (own funds requirements), and transitional provisions and is structures in five templates:
>
> - a)) CA1 template contains the amount of own funds of the institutions, disaggregated in the items needed to get to that amount. The amount of own funds obtained includes the aggregate effect of transitional provisions per type of capital
> - b)) CA2 template summarizes the total risk exposures amounts as defined in Article 92(3) of Regulation (EU) No 575/2013 (“CRR”)
> - c)) CA3 template contains the ratios for which CRR state a minimum level, and some other related data
> - d)) CA4 template contains memorandums items needed for calculating items in CA1 as well as information with regard to the CRD capital buffers.
> - e)) CA5 template contains the data needed for calculating the effect of transitional provisions in own funds. CA5 will cease to exist once the transitional provisions will expire.
>
> 11\.
>
> The templates shall apply to all reporting entities, irrespective of the accounting standards followed, although some items in the numerator are specific for entities applying IAS/IFRS-type valuation rules. Generally, the information in the denominator is linked to the final results reported in the correspondent templates for the calculation of the total risk exposure amount.
>
> 12\.
>
> The total own funds consist of different types of capital: Tier 1 capital (T1), which is the sum of Common Equity Tier 1 capital (CET1), Additional Tier 1 capital (AT1) as well as Tier 2 capital (T2).
>
> 13\.
>
> Transitional provisions are treated as follows in CA templates:
>
> - a)) The items in CA1 are generally gross of transitional adjustments. This means that figures in CA1 items are calculated according to the *final provisions* (i.e. as if there were no transitional provisions), with the exception of items summarizing the effect of the transitional provisions. For each type of capital (i.e. CET1; AT1 and T2) there are three different items in which all the adjustments due to transitional provisions are included.
> - b)) Transitional provisions may also affect the AT1 and the T2 shortfall (i.e. AT1 or T2 the excess of deduction, regulated in articles 36(1) point (j) and 56 point (e) of CRR respectively), and thus the items containing these shortfalls may indirectly reflect the effect of transitional provisions.
> - c)) Template CA5 is exclusively used for reporting the transitional provisions.
>
> 14\.
>
> The treatment of Pillar II requirements can be different within the EU (Article 104(2) CRD IV has to be transposed into national regulation). Only the impact of Pillar II requirements on the solvency ratio or the target ratio shall be included in the solvency reporting of CRR. A detailed reporting of Pillar II requirements is not within the mandate of Article 99 CRR.
>
> - a)) The templates CA1, CA2 or CA5 only contain data on Pillar I issues.
> - b)) The template CA3 contains the impact of additional Pillar II-requirements on the solvency ratio on an aggregated basis. One block focuses on the impact of amounts on the ratios, whereas the other block focuses on the ratio itself. Both blocks of ratios do not have any further link to the templates CA1, CA2 or CA5.
> - c)) The template CA4 contains one cell regarding additional own funds requirements relating to Pillar II. This cell has no link via validation rules to the capital ratios of the CA3 template and reflects Article 104(2) CRD which explicitly mentions additional own funds requirements as one possibility for Pillar II decisions.
>
> 1.2.
>
> C 01.00 — OWN FUNDS (CA1)
>
> 1.2.1.
>

> | 720 | 1.1.2.8<br>**(-) Excess of deduction from T2 items over T2 Capital**<br>Article 56 point (e) of CRR<br>The amount to be reported is directly taken from CA 1 item “Excess of deduction from T2 items over T2 Capital (deducted in AT1)”. |
> | 730 | 1.1.2.9<br>**Other transitional adjustments to AT1 Capital**<br>Articles 474, 475, 478 and 481 of CRR<br>Adjustments due to transitional provisions. The amount to be reported is directly obtained from CA5. |
> | 740 | 1.1.2.10<br>**Excess of deduction from AT1 items over AT1 Capital (deducted in CET1)**<br>Article 36(1) point (j) of CRR<br>Additional Tier 1 cannot be negative, but it is possible that AT1 deductions are greater than AT1 Capital plus related share premium. When this happens, AT1 has to be equal to zero, and the excess of AT1 deductions has to be deducted from CET1.<br>With this item, it is achieved that the sum of items 1.1.2.1 to 1.1.2.12 is never lower than zero. Then, if this item shows a positive figure, item 1.1.1.16 shall be the inverse of that figure. |
> | 744 | 1.1.2.11<br>**Additional deductions of AT1 Capital due to Article 3 CRR**<br>Article 3 CRR |
> | 748 | 1.1.2.12<br>**AT1 capital elements or deductions — other**<br>This row is invented to provide flexibility solely for reporting purposes. It shall only be populated in the rare cases that there is no final decision on the reporting of specific capital items/deductions in the current CA1 template. As a consequence, this row shall only be populated if an AT1 capital element respective a deduction of an AT1 element cannot be assigned to one of the rows 530 to 744.<br>This cell shall not be used to assign capital items/deductions which are not covered by the CRR into the calculation of solvency ratios (e.g. an assignment of national capital items/deductions which are outside the scope of the CRR)! |
> | 750 | 1.2<br>**TIER 2 CAPITAL**<br>Article 71 of CRR |
> | 760 | 1.2.1<br>**Capital instruments and subordinated loans eligible as T2 Capital**<br>Articles 62 point (a), 63 to 65, 66 point (a), and 67 of CRR |
> | 770 | 1.2.1.1<br>**Paid up capital instruments and subordinated loans**<br>Articles 62 point (a), 63 and 65 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported shall not include the share premium related to the instruments |
> | 780 | 1.2.1.2 (\*)<br>**Memorandum item: Capital instruments and subordinated loans not eligible**<br>Article 63 points (c), (e) and (f); and article 64 of CRR<br>Conditions in those points reflect different situations of the capital which are reversible, and thus the amount reported here can be eligible in subsequent periods.<br>The amount to be reported shall not include the share premium related to the instruments |
> | 790 | 1.2.1.3<br>**Share premium**<br>Articles 62 point (b) and 65 of CRR<br>Share premium has the same meaning as under the applicable accounting standard.<br>The amount to be reported in this item shall be the part related to the “Paid up capital instruments”. |
> | 800 | 1.2.1.4<br>**(-) Own T2 instruments**<br>Article 63 point (b) (i), 66 point (a), and 67 of CRR<br>Own T2 instruments held by the reporting institution or group at the reporting date. Subject to exceptions in article 67 of CRR.<br>Holdings on shares included as “Capital instruments not eligible” shall not be reported in this row.<br>The amount to be reported shall include the share premium related to the own shares.<br>Items 1.2.1.4 to 1.2.1.4.3 do not include actual or contingent obligations to purchase own T2 instruments. Actual or contingent obligations to purchase own T2 instruments are reported separately in item 1.2.1.5. |
> | 810 | 1.2.1.4.1<br>**(-) Direct holdings of T2 instruments**<br>Articles 63 point (b), 66 point (a) and 67 of CRR<br>Tier 2 instruments included in item 1.2.1.1 held by institutions of the consolidated group. |
> | 840 | 1.2.1.4.2<br>**(-) Indirect holdings of T2 instruments**<br>Articles 4(1)(114), 63 point (b), 66 point (a) and 67 of CRR |
> | 841 | 1.2.1.4.3<br>**(-) Synthetic holdings of T2 instruments**<br>Articles 4(1)(126), 63 point (b), 66 point (a) and 67 of CRR |
> | 842 | 1.2.1.5<br>**(-) Actual or contingent obligations to purchase own T2 instruments**<br>Articles 66 point (a) and 67 of CRR<br>According to Article 66 point (a) of CRR, “own Tier 2 instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase as a result of existing contractual obligations” shall be deducted. |
> | 880 | 1.2.2<br>**Transitional adjustments due to grandfathered T2 Capital instruments and subordinated loans**<br>Articles 483(6) and (7), 484, 486, 488, 490 and 491 of CRR<br>Amount of capital instruments transitionally grandfathered as T2. The amount to be reported is directly obtained from CA5. |
> | 890 | 1.2.3<br>**Instruments issued by subsidiaries that are given recognition in T2 Capital**<br>Articles 83, 87 and 88 of CRR<br>Sum of all the amounts of qualifying own funds of subsidiaries that is included in consolidated T2.<br>Qualifying Tier 2 capital issued by a special purpose entity (Article 83 of CRR) shall be included. |
> | 900 | 1.2.4<br>**Transitional adjustments due to additional recognition in T2 Capital of instruments issued by subsidiaries**<br>Article 480 of CRR<br>Adjustments to the qualifying own funds included in consolidated T2 capital due to transitional provisions. This item is obtained directly from CA5. |
> | 910 | 1.2.5<br>**IRB Excess of provisions over expected losses eligible**<br>Article 62 point (d) of CRR<br>For institutions calculating risk-weighted exposure amounts in accordance with IRB approach, this item contains the positive amounts resulting from comparing the provisions and expected losses which are eligible as T2 capital. |
> | 920 | 1.2.6<br>**SA General credit risk adjustments**<br>Article 62 point (c) of CRR<br>For institutions calculating risk-weighted exposure amounts in accordance with standard approach, this item contains the general credit risk adjustments eligible as T2 capital. |
> | 930 | 1.2.7<br>**(-) Reciprocal cross holdings in T2 Capital**<br>Articles 4(1)(122), 66 point (b) and 68 of CRR<br>Holdings in T2 instruments of financial sector entities (as defined in Article 4(1)(27) of CRR) where there is a reciprocal cross holding that the competent authority considers to have been designed to inflate artificially the own funds of the institution.<br>The amount to be reported shall be calculated on the basis of the gross long positions, and shall include Tier 2 and Tier 3 own-fund insurance items. |
> | 940 | 1.2.8<br>**(-) T2 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment**<br>Articles 4(1)(27), 66 point (c), 68 to 70 and 79 of CRR<br>Part of holdings by the institution of instruments of financial sector entities (as defined in Article 4(1)(27) of CRR) where the institution does not have a significant investment that has to be deducted from T2. |
> | 950 | 1.2.9<br>**(-) T2 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment**<br>Articles 4(1)(27), 66 point (d), 68, 69 and 79 of CRR<br>Holdings by the institution of T2 instruments of financial sector entities (as defined in Article 4(1)(27) of CRR) where the institution has a significant investment shall be completely deducted. |
> | 960 | 1.2.10<br>**Other transitional adjustments to T2 Capital**<br>Articles 476 to 478 and 481 of CRR<br>Adjustments due to transitional provisions. The amount to be reported shall be directly obtained from CA5. |
> | 970 | 1.2.11<br>**Excess of deduction from T2 items over T2 Capital (deducted in AT1)**<br>Article 56 point (e) of CRR<br>Tier 2 cannot be negative, but it is possible that T2 deductions are greater than T2 Capital plus related share premium. When this happens, T2 shall be equal to zero, and the excess of T2 deductions shall be deducted from AT1.<br>With this item, the sum of items 1.2.1 to 1.2.13 is never lower than zero. If this item shows a positive figure, item 1.1.2.8 shall be the inverse of that figure. |
> | 974 | 1.2.12<br>**(-) Additional deductions of T2 Capital due to Article 3 CRR**<br>Article 3 CRR |
> | 978 | 1.2.13<br>**T2 capital elements or deductions — other**<br>This row is invented to provide flexibility solely for reporting purposes. It shall only be populated in the rare cases that there is no final decision on the reporting of specific capital items/deductions in the current CA1 template. As a consequence, this row shall only be populated if a T2 capital element respective a deduction of a T2 element cannot be assigned to one of the rows 750 to 974.<br>This cell shall not be used to assign capital items/deductions which are not covered by the CRR into the calculation of solvency ratios (e.g. an assignment of national capital items/deductions which are outside the scope of the CRR). |

> 1.4.
>
> C 03.00 — CAPITAL RATIOS AND CAPITAL LEVELS (CA3)
>
> 1.4.1.
>
> Instructions concerning specific positions
>
> <table>
> <tbody>
> <tr><th colspan="2">Rows</th></tr>
> <tr><td>010</td><td>1<br><strong>CET1 Capital ratio</strong><br>Article 92(2) point (a) of CRR<br>The CET1 capital ratio is the CET1 capital of the institution expressed as a percentage of the total risk exposure amount.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>020</td><td>2<br><strong>Surplus(+)/Deficit(–) of CET1 capital</strong><br>This item shows, in absolute figures, the amount of CET1 capital surplus or deficit relating to the requirement set in Article 92(1) point (a) of CRR (4,5 %), i.e. without taking into account the capital buffers and transitional provisions on the ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>030</td><td>3<br><strong>T1 Capital ratio</strong><br>Article 92(2) point (b) of CRR<br>The T1 capital ratio is the T1 capital of the institution expressed as a percentage of the total risk exposure amount.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>040</td><td>4<br><strong>Surplus(+)/Deficit(–) of T1 capital</strong><br>This item shows, in absolute figures, the amount of T1 capital surplus or deficit relating to the requirement set in Article 92(1) point (b) of CRR (6 %), i.e. without taking into account the capital buffers and transitional provisions on the ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>050</td><td>5<br><strong>Total capital ratio</strong><br>Article 92(2) point (c) of CRR<br>The total capital ratio is the own funds of the institution expressed as a percentage of the total risk exposure amount.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>060</td><td>6<br><strong>Surplus(+)/Deficit(–) of total capital</strong><br>This item shows, in absolute figures, the amount of own funds surplus or deficit relating to the requirement set in Article 92(1) point (c) of CRR (8 %), i.e. without taking into account the capital buffers and transitional provisions on the ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>070</td><td><strong>CET1 capital ratio including Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 92(2) point (a) of CRR and Article 104(2) CRD IV<br>This cell only has to be populated if a decision of a competent authority has an impact on the CET1 capital ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>080</td><td><strong>Target CET1 capital ratio due to Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 104(2) CRD IV<br>This cell only has to be populated if a competent authority decides that an institution has to meet a higher target CET1capital ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>090</td><td><strong>T1 capital ratio including Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 92(2) point (b) of CRR and Article 104(2) CRD IV<br>This cell only has to be populated if a decision of a competent authority has an impact on the T1 capital ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>100</td><td><strong>Target T1 capital ratio due to Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 104(2) CRD IV<br>This cell only has to be populated if a competent authority decides that an institution has to meet a higher target T1 capital ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>110</td><td><strong>Total capital ratio including Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 92(2) point (c) of CRR and Article 104(2) CRD IV<br>This cell only has to be populated if a decision of a competent authority has an impact on the total capital ratio.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>120</td><td><strong>Target Total capital ratio due to Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 104(2) CRD IV<br>This cell only has to be populated if a competent authority decides that an institution has to meet a higher target total capital ratio.</td></tr>
> </tbody>
> </table>
>
> 1.5.
>
> C 04.00 — MEMORANDUM ITEMS (CA4)
>
> 1.5.1.
>
> Instructions concerning specific positions
>
> <table>
> <tbody>
> <tr><th colspan="2">Rows</th></tr>
> <tr><td>010</td><td>1.<br><strong>Total deferred tax assets</strong><br>The amount reported in this item shall be equal to the amount reported in the latest verified/audited accounting balance sheet.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>020</td><td>1.1<br><strong>Deferred tax assets that do not rely on future profitability</strong><br>Article 39 of CRR<br>Deferred tax assets that do not rely on future profitability, and thus are subject to the application of a risk weight.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>030</td><td>1.2<br><strong>Deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and do not arise from temporary differences</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (c) and 38 of CRR<br>Deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability, but do not arise from temporary differences, and thus are not subject to any threshold (i.e. are completely deducted from CET1).</td></tr>
> <tr><td>040</td><td>1.3<br><strong>Deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and arise from temporary differences</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (c); 38 and 48(1) point (a) of CRR<br>Deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and arise from temporary differences, and thus, their deduction from CET1 is subject to 10 % and 17,65 % thresholds in Article 48 of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>050</td><td>2<br><strong>Total deferred tax liabilities</strong><br>The amount reported in this item shall be equal to the amount reported in the latest verified/audited accounting balance sheet.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>060</td><td>2.1<br><strong>Deferred tax liabilities non deductible from deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability</strong><br>Article 38(3) and (4) of CRR<br>Deferred tax liabilities for which conditions in Article 38(3) and (4) of CRR are not met. Hence, this item shall include the deferred tax liabilities that reduce the amount of goodwill, other intangible assets or defined benefit pension fund assets required to be deducted, which are reported, respectively, in CA1 items 1.1.1.10.3, 1.1.1.11.2 and 1.1.1.14.2.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>070</td><td>2.2<br><strong>Deferred tax liabilities deductible from deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability</strong><br>Article 38 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>080</td><td>2.2.1<br><strong>Deductible deferred tax liabilities associated with deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and do not arise from temporary differences</strong><br>Article 38(3), (4) and (5) of CRR<br>Deferred tax liabilities which may reduce the amount of deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability, according to Article 38(3) and (4) of CRR, and are not allocated to deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and arise from temporary differences, according to Article 38(5) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>090</td><td>2.2.2<br><strong>Deductible deferred tax liabilities associated with deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and arise from temporary differences</strong><br>Article 38(3), (4) and (5) of CRR<br>Deferred tax liabilities which may reduce the amount of deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability, according to Article 38(3) and (4) of CRR, and are allocated to deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and arise from temporary differences, according to Article 38(5) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>100</td><td>3.<br><strong>IRB excess (+) or shortfall (–) of credit risk adjustments, additional value adjustments and other own funds reductions to expected losses for non defaulted exposures</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (d), 62 point (d), 158 and 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>110</td><td>3.1<br><strong>Total credit risk adjustments, additional value adjustments and other own funds reductions eligible for inclusion in the calculation of the expected loss amount</strong><br>Article 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>120</td><td>3.1.1<br><strong>General credit risk adjustments</strong><br>Article 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>130</td><td>3.1.2<br><strong>Specific credit risk adjustments</strong><br>Article 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>131</td><td>3.1.3<br><strong>Additional value adjustments and other own funds reductions</strong><br>Articles 34, 110 and 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>140</td><td>3.2<br><strong>Total expected losses eligible</strong><br>Articles 158(5), (6) and (10), and 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions. Only the expected loss related to non defaulted exposures shall be reported.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>145</td><td>4<br><strong>IRB excess (+) or shortfall (–) of specific credit risk adjustments to expected losses for defaulted exposures</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (d), 62 point (d), 158 and 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>150</td><td>4.1<br><strong>Specific credit risk adjustments and positions treated similarily</strong><br>Article 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>155</td><td>4.2<br><strong>Total expected losses eligible</strong><br>Articles 158(5), (6) and (10), and 159 of CRR<br>This item shall only be reported by IRB institutions. Only the expected loss related to defaulted exposures shall be reported.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>160</td><td>5<br><strong>Risk weighted exposure amounts for calculating the cap to the excess of provision eligible as T2</strong><br>Article 62 point (d) of CRR<br>For IRB institutions, according to Article 62 point (d) of CRR, the excess amount of provisions (to expected losses) eligible for inclusion in Tier 2 capital is capped at 0,6 % of risk-weighted exposure amounts calculated with the IRB approach.<br>The amount to be reported in this item is the risk weighted exposure amounts (i.e. not multiplied by 0,6 %) which is the base for calculating the cap.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>170</td><td>6<br><strong>Total gross provisions eligible for inclusion in T2 capital</strong><br>Article 62 point (c) of CRR<br>This item includes the general credit risk adjustments that are eligible for inclusion in T2 capital, before cap.<br>The amount to be reported shall be gross of tax effects.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>180</td><td>7<br><strong>Risk weighted exposure amounts for calculating the cap to the provision eligible as T2</strong><br>Article 62 point (c) of CRR<br>According to Article 62 point (c) of CRR, the credit risk adjustments eligible for inclusion in Tier 2 capital is capped at 1,25 % of risk-weighted exposure amounts.<br>The amount to be reported in this item is the risk weighted exposure amounts (i.e. not multiplied by 1,25 %) which is the base for calculating the cap.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>190</td><td>8<br><strong>Threshold non deductible of holdings in financial sector entities where an institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Article 46(1) point (a) of CRR<br>This item contains the threshold up to which holdings in a financial sector entity where an institution does not have a significant investment are not deducted. The amount results from adding up all items which are the base of the threshold and multiplying the sum thus obtained by 10 %.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>200</td><td>9<br><strong>10 % CET1 threshold</strong><br>Article 48(1) points (a) and (b) of CRR<br>This item contains the 10 % threshold for holdings in financial sector entities where an institution has a significant investment, and for deferred tax assets that are dependent on future profitability and arise from temporary differences.<br>The amount results from adding up all items which are the base of the threshold and multiplying the sum thus obtained by 10 %.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>210</td><td>10<br><strong>17,65 % CET1 threshold</strong><br>Article 48(1) of CRR<br>This item contains the 17,65 % threshold for holdings in financial sector entities where an institution has a significant investment, and for deferred tax assets that are dependent on future profitability and arise from temporary differences, to be applied after the 10 % threshold.<br>The threshold is calculated so that the amount of the two items that is recognised must not exceed 15 % of the Common Equity Tier 1 capital, calculated after all deductions, not including any adjustment due to transitional provisions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>225</td><td>11.1<br><strong>Eligible capital for the purposes of qualifying holdings outside the financial sector</strong><br>Article 4(1)(71)(a)</td></tr>
> <tr><td>226</td><td>11.2<br><strong>Eligible capital for the purposes of large exposures</strong><br>Article 4(1)(71)(b)</td></tr>
> <tr><td>230</td><td>12<br><strong>Holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment, net of short positions</strong><br>Articles 44 to 46 and 49 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>240</td><td>12.1<br><strong>Direct holdings of CET1 capital Direct holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 44, 45, 46 and 49 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>250</td><td>12.1.1<br><strong>Gross direct holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 44, 46 and 49 of CRR<br>Direct holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment, excluding:<br>a)) Underwriting positions held for 5 working days or fewer;<br>b)) The amounts relating to the investments for which any alternative in article 49 is applied; and<br>c)) Holdings which are treated as reciprocal cross holdings according to article 36(1) point (g) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>260</td><td>12.1.2<br><strong>(-) Permitted offsetting short positions in relation to the direct gross holdings included above</strong><br>Article 45 of CRR<br>Article 45 of CRR allows offsetting short positions in the same underlying exposure provided the maturity of the short position matches the maturity of the long position or has a residual maturity of at least one year.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>270</td><td>12.2<br><strong>Indirect holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 4(1)(114), 44 and 45 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>280</td><td>12.2.1<br><strong>Gross indirect holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 4(1)(114), 44 and 45 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported is the indirect holdings in the trading book of the capital instruments of financial sector entities that take the form of holdings of index securities. It is obtained by calculating the underlying exposure to the capital instruments of the financial sector entities in the indices.<br>Holdings which are treated as reciprocal cross holdings according to article 36(1) point (g) of CRR shall not be included</td></tr>
> <tr><td>290</td><td>12.2.2<br><strong>(-) Permitted offsetting short positions in relation to the indirect gross holdings included above</strong><br>Articles 4(1)(114) and 45 of CRR<br>Article 45 point (a) of CRR allows offsetting short positions in the same underlying exposure provided the maturity of the short position matches the maturity of the long position or has a residual maturity of at least one year.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>291</td><td>12.3.1<br><strong>Synthetic holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 4(1)(126), 44 and 45 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>292</td><td>12.3.2<br><strong>Gross synthetic holdings of CET1 capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 4(1)(126), 44 and 45 of CRR</td></tr>

> <tr><td>680</td><td>21<br><strong>Holdings on CET1 Capital Instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment temporary waived</strong><br>Article 79 of CRR<br>A competent authority may waive on a temporary basis the provisions on deductions from CET1 due to holdings on instruments of a specific financial sector entity, when it deems those holdings to be for the purposes of a financial assistance operation designed to reorganise and save that entity.<br>Note that these instruments shall also be reported on item 12.1.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>690</td><td>22<br><strong>Holdings on CET1 Capital Instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment temporary waived</strong><br>Article 79 of CRR<br>A competent authority may waive on a temporary basis the provisions on deductions from CET1 due to holdings on instruments of a specific financial sector entity, when it deems those holdings to be for the purposes of a financial assistance operation designed to reorganise and save that entity.<br>Note that these instruments shall also be reported on item 15.1.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>700</td><td>23<br><strong>Holdings on AT1 Capital Instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment temporary waived</strong><br>Article 79 of CRR<br>A competent authority may waive on a temporary basis the provisions on deductions from AT1 due to holdings on instruments of a specific financial sector entity, when it deems those holdings to be for the purposes of a financial assistance operation designed to reorganise and save that entity.<br>Note that these instruments shall also be reported on item 13.1.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>710</td><td>24<br><strong>Holdings on AT1 Capital Instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment temporary waived</strong><br>Article 79 of CRR<br>A competent authority may waive on a temporary basis the provisions on deductions from AT1 due to holdings on instruments of a specific financial sector entity, when it deems those holdings to be for the purposes of a financial assistance operation designed to reorganise and save that entity.<br>Note that these instruments shall also be reported on item 16.1.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>720</td><td>25<br><strong>Holdings on T2 Capital Instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment temporary waived</strong><br>Article 79 of CRR<br>A competent authority may waive on a temporary basis the provisions on deductions from T2 due to holdings on instruments of a specific financial sector entity, when it deems those holdings to be for the purposes of a financial assistance operation designed to reorganise and save that entity.<br>Note that these instruments shall also be reported on item 14.1.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>730</td><td>26<br><strong>Holdings on T2 Capital Instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment temporary waived</strong><br>Article 79 of CRR<br>A competent authority may waive on a temporary basis the provisions on deductions from T2 due to holdings on instruments of a specific financial sector entity, when it deems those holdings to be for the purposes of a financial assistance operation designed to reorganise and save that entity.<br>Note that these instruments shall also be reported on item 17.1.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>740</td><td>27<br><strong>Combined buffer requirement</strong><br>Article 128 point (6) of CRD</td></tr>
> <tr><td>750</td><td><strong>Capital conservation buffer</strong><br>Articles 128 point (1) and 129 of CRD<br>According to Article 129(1) the capital conservation buffer is an additional amount of Common Equity Tier 1 capital. Due to the fact that the capital conservation buffer rate of 2,5 % is stable, an amount shall be reported in this cell.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>760</td><td><strong>Conservation buffer due to macro-prudential or systemic risk identified at the level of a Member State</strong><br>Article 458(2) point d (iv) of CRR<br>In this cell the amount of the conservation buffer due to macro-prudential or systemic risk identified at the level of a Member State, which can be requested according to Article 458 CRR in addition to the capital conservation buffer shall be reported.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>770</td><td><strong>Institution specific countercyclical capital buffer</strong><br>Articles 128 point (2), 130, 135-140 of CRD</td></tr>
> <tr><td>780</td><td><strong>Systemic risk buffer</strong><br>Articles 128 point (5), 133 and 134 of CRD</td></tr>
> <tr><td>790</td><td><strong>Systemically important institution buffer</strong><br>Article 131 of CRD<br>Institutions shall report the amount of the Systemically important institution buffer which is applicable on a consolidated basis.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>800</td><td><strong>Global Systemically Important Institution buffer</strong><br>Articles 128 point (3) and 131 of CRD</td></tr>
> <tr><td>810</td><td><strong>Other Systemically Important Institution buffer</strong><br>Articles 128 point (4) and 131 of CRD</td></tr>
> <tr><td>820</td><td>28<br><strong>Own funds requirements related to Pillar II adjustments</strong><br>Article 104(2) of CRD.<br>If a competent authority decides that an institution has to calculate additional own funds requirements for Pillar II reasons, those additional own funds requirements shall be reported in this cell.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>830</td><td>29<br><strong>Initial capital</strong><br>Articles 12, 28 to 31of CRD and Article 93 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>840</td><td>30<br><strong>Own funds based on Fixed Overheads</strong><br>Articles 96(2) point (b), 97 and 98(1) point (a) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>850</td><td>31<br><strong>Non-domestic original exposures</strong><br>Information necessary to calculate the threshold for reporting of the CR GB template according to Article 5(a)(4) of ITS. The calculation of the threshold shall be done at the basis of the original exposure pre conversion factor.<br>Exposures shall be deemed to be domestic where they are exposures to counterparties located in the Member State where the institution is located.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>860</td><td>32<br><strong>Total original exposures</strong><br>Information necessary to calculate the threshold for reporting of the CR GB template according to Article 5(a)(4) of ITS. The calculation of the threshold shall be done at the basis of the original exposure pre conversion factor.<br>Exposures shall be deemed to be domestic where they are exposures to counterparties located in the Member State where the institution is located.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>870</td><td><strong>Adjustments to total own funds</strong><br>Article 500(4) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>880</td><td><strong>Own funds fully adjusted for Basel I floor</strong><br>Article 500(1) point (b) and (4) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>890</td><td><strong>Own funds requirements for Basel I floor</strong><br>Article 500(1) point (b) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>900</td><td><strong>Own funds requirements for Basel I floor — SA alternative</strong><br>Article 500(2) and (3) of CRR</td></tr>
> </tbody>
> </table>
>
> 1.6.
>
> TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS AND GRANDFATHERED INSTRUMENTS: INSTRUMENTS NOT CONSTITUTING STATE AID (CA 5)
>
> 1.6.1.
>
> General remarks
>
> 15\.
>
> CA5 summarizes the calculation of own funds elements and deductions subject to the transitional provisions laid down in Articles 465 to 491 of CRR.
>
> 16\.
>
> CA5 is structured as follows:
>
> - a.. Template 5.1 summarizes the total adjustments which need to be made to the different components of own funds (reported in CA1 according to the final provisions) as a consequence of the application of the transitional provisions. The elements of this table are presented as “adjustments” to the different capital components in CA1, in order to reflect in own funds components the effects of the transitional provisions.
> - b.. Template 5.2 provides further details on the calculation of those grandfathered instruments which do not constitute state aid.
>
> 17\.
>
> Institutions shall report in the first four columns the adjustments to Common Equity Tier 1 capital, Additional Tier 1 capital and Tier 2 capital as well as the amount to be treated as risk weighted assets. Institutions are also required to report the applicable percentage in column 050 and the eligible amount without the recognition of transitional provisions in column 060.
>
> 18\.
>
> Institutions shall only report elements in CA5 during the period where transitional provisions in accordance with Part Ten of CRR apply.
>
> 19\.
>
> Some of the transitional provisions require a deduction from Tier 1. If this is the case the residual amount of a deduction or deductions is applied to Tier 1 and there is insufficient AT1 to absorb this amount then the excess shall be deducted from CET1.
>
> 1.6.2.
>
> C 05.01 — Transitional provisions (CA5.1)
>
> 20\.
>
> Institutions shall report in Table 5.1 the transitional provisions to own funds components as laid down in Articles 465 to 491 of CRR, compared to applying the final provisions laid down in Title II of Part Two of CRR.
>
> 21\.
>
> Institutions shall report in rows 020 to 060 information in relation with the transitional provisions of grandfathered instruments. The figures to be reported in columns 010 to 030 of row 060 of CA 5.1 can be derived from the respective sections of CA 5.2.
>
> 22\.
>
> Institutions shall report in rows 070 to 092 information in relation with the transitional provisions of minority interests and additional Tier 1 and Tier 2 instruments issued by subsidiaries (in accordance with Articles 479 and 480 of CRR).
>
> 23\.
>
> In rows 100 onwards institutions shall report information in relation with the transitional provisions of unrealized gains and losses, deductions as well as additional filters and deductions.
>
> 24\.
>
> There might be cases where the transitional deductions of CET1, AT1 or T2 capital exceed the CET1, AT1 or T2 capital of an institution. This effect — if it results from transitional provisions — shall be shown in the CA1 template using the respective cells. As a consequence, the adjustments in the columns of the CA5 template do not include any spill-over effects in the case of insufficient capital available.
>
> 1.6.2.1.
>
> Instructions concerning specific positions
>
> <table>
> <tbody>
> <tr><th colspan="2">Columns</th></tr>
> <tr><td>010</td><td><strong>Adjustments to CET1</strong></td></tr>
> <tr><td>020</td><td><strong>Adjustments to AT1</strong></td></tr>
> <tr><td>030</td><td><strong>Adjustments to T2</strong></td></tr>
> <tr><td>040</td><td><strong>Adjustments included in RWAs</strong><br>Column 050 includes the relevant residual amount, i.e. prior the application of provisions of Chapter 2 or 3 of Part Three of CRR.<br>Whereas columns 010 to 030 have a direct link to the CA1 template, the adjustments included in RWA do not have a direct link to the relevant templates for credit risk. If there are adjustments stemming from the transitional provisions to the RWA, those adjustments shall be included directly in the CR SA, CR IRB or CR EQU IRB. Additionally, those effects shall be reported in column 040 of CA5.1. As a consequence, those amounts are only memorandum items.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>050</td><td><strong>Applicable percentage</strong></td></tr>
> <tr><td>060</td><td><strong>Eligible amount without transitional provisions</strong><br>Column 060 includes the amount of each instrument prior the application of transitional provisions. I.e. the basis amount relevant to calculate the adjustments.</td></tr>
> </tbody>
> </table>
>
> <table>
> <tbody>
> <tr><th colspan="2">Rows</th></tr>
> <tr><td>010</td><td>1.<br><strong>Total adjustments</strong><br>This row reflects the overall effect of transitional adjustments in the different types of capital, plus the risk weighted amounts arising from these adjustments</td></tr>
> <tr><td>020</td><td>1.1<br><strong>Grandfathered instruments</strong><br>Articles 483 to 491 of CRR<br>This row reflects the overall effect of instruments transitionally grandfathered in the different types of capital.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>030</td><td>1.1.1<br><strong>Grandfathered instruments: Instruments constituting state aid</strong><br>Article 483 CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>040</td><td>1.1.1.1<br><strong>Instruments that qualified as own funds according to 2006/48/EC</strong><br>Article 483(1) (2), (4) and (6) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>050</td><td>1.1.1.2<br><strong>Instruments issued by institutions that are incorporated in a Member State that is subject to an Economic Adjustment Programme</strong><br>Article 483(1), (3), (5), (7) and (8) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>060</td><td>1.1.2<br><strong>Instruments not constituting state aid</strong><br>The amounts to be reported shall be obtained from column 060 of table CA 5.2.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>070</td><td>1.2<br><strong>Minority interests and equivalents</strong><br>Articles 479 and 480 of CRR<br>This row reflects the effects of transitional provisions in the minority interests eligible as CET1; the qualifying T1 instruments eligible as consolidated AT1; and the qualifying own funds eligible as consolidated T2.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>080</td><td>1.2.1<br><strong>Capital instruments and items that do not qualify as minority interests</strong><br>Articles 479 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row shall be the amount qualifying as consolidated reserves in accordance with prior regulation.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>090</td><td>1.2.2<br><strong>Transitional recognition in consolidated own funds of minority interests</strong><br>Articles 84 and 480 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row shall be the eligible amount without transitional provisions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>091</td><td>1.2.3<br><strong>Transitional recognition in consolidated own funds of qualifying Additional Tier 1 capital</strong><br>Article 85 and 480 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row shall be the eligible amount without transitional provisions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>092</td><td>1.2.4<br><strong>Transitional recognition in consolidated own funds of qualifying Tier 2 capital</strong><br>Article 87 and 480 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row shall be the eligible amount without transitional provisions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>100</td><td>1.3<br><strong>Other transitional adjustments</strong><br>Articles 467 to 478 and 481 of CRR<br>This row reflects the overall effect of transitional adjustments in the deduction to different types of capital, unrealised gains and losses, additional filters and deductions plus the risk weighted amounts arising from these adjustments.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>110</td><td>1.3.1<br><strong>Unrealised gains and losses</strong><br>Articles 467 and 468 of CRR<br>This row reflects the overall effect of transitional provisions on unrealized gains and losses measured at fair value.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>120</td><td>1.3.1.1<br><strong>Unrealised gains</strong><br>Article 468(1) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>130</td><td>1.3.1.2<br><strong>Unrealised losses</strong><br>Article 467(1) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>133</td><td>1.3.1.3<br><strong>Unrealised gains on exposures to central governments classified in the “Available for sale” category of EU-endorsed IAS39</strong><br>Article 468 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>136</td><td>1.3.1.4<br><strong>Unrealised loss on exposures to central governments classified in the “Available for sale” category of EU-endorsed IAS39</strong><br>Article 467 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>138</td><td>1.3.1.5<br><strong>Fair value gains and losses arising from the institution's own credit risk related to derivative liabilities</strong><br>Article 468 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>140</td><td>1.3.2<br><strong>Deductions</strong><br>Articles 36(1), 469 to 478 of CRR<br>This row reflects the overall effect of transitional provisions on deductions.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>150</td><td>1.3.2.1.<br><strong>Losses for the current financial year</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (a), 469 (1), 472 (3) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row shall be the original deduction according to Article 36(1)(a) of CRR.<br>Where firms have only been required to deduct material losses:<br>where the total interim net loss was “material”, the full residual amount would be deducted from Tier 1, or<br>where the whole total interim net loss was not “material”, no deduction of residual amount would be made.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>160</td><td>1.3.2.2.<br><strong>Intangible assets</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (b), 469 (1), 472 (4) and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of intangible assets to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 37 of CRR.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row shall be the original deduction Article 36(1)(b) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>170</td><td>1.3.2.3.<br><strong>Deferred tax assets that rely on future profitability and do not arise from temporary differences</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (c), 469 (1), 472 (5) and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of the above-mentioned deferred tax assets (DTA) to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 38 of CRR relating to the reduction of DTA by deferred tax liabilities.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount according to Article 469(1) c) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>180</td><td>1.3.2.4.<br><strong>IRB shortfall of provisions to expected losses</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (d), 469 (1), 472 (6) and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of the above-mentioned IRB shortfall of provisions to expected losses to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 40 of CRR.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(d) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>190</td><td>1.3.2.5.<br><strong>Defined benefit pension fund assets</strong><br>Articles 33(1) point (e), 469 (1), 472 (7), 473 and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of the above-mentioned defined benefit pension fund assets to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 41 of CRR.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(e) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>194</td><td>1.3.2.5.*<br><strong>of which: Introduction of amendments to IAS 19 — positive item</strong><br>Article 473 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>198</td><td>1.3.2.5.**<br><strong>of which: Introduction of amendments to IAS 19 — negative item</strong><br>Article 473 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>200</td><td>1.3.2.6.<br><strong>Own instruments</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (f), 469 (1), 472 (8) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(f) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>210</td><td>1.3.2.6.1<br><strong>Own CET1 instruments</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (f), 469 (1), 472 (8) and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of the above-mentioned Own Common Equity Tier 1 instruments to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 42 of CRR.<br>Given that the treatment of the “residual amount” differs depending upon the nature of the instrument, institutions shall break down holdings in own Common Equity instruments according to “direct” and “indirect” holdings.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(f) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>211</td><td>1.3.2.6.1**<br><strong>of which: Direct holdings</strong><br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount of direct holdings, including instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase by virtue of an existing or contingent contractual obligation, Article 469(1)(b), 472 (8) (a) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>212</td><td>1.3.2.6.1*<br><strong>of which: Indirect holdings</strong><br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount of indirect holdings, including instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase by virtue of an existing or contingent contractual obligation, Article 469(1)(b), 472 (8) (b) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>220</td><td>1.3.2.6.2<br><strong>Own AT1 instruments</strong><br>Articles 56 point (a), 474, 475(2) and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of the above-mentioned holdings to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 57 of CRR.<br>Given that the treatment of the “residual amount” differs depending upon the nature of the instrument (Article 475(2) of CRR), institutions shall break down the above-mentioned holdings according to “direct” and “indirect” own Additional Tier 1 holdings.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 56 (a) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>221</td><td>1.3.2.6.2**<br><strong>of which: Direct holdings</strong><br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount of direct holdings, including instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase by virtue of an existing or contingent contractual obligation, Articles 474 (b) and 475 (2) (a) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>222</td><td>1.3.2.6.2*<br><strong>of which: Indirect holdings</strong><br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount of indirect holdings, including instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase by virtue of an existing or contingent contractual obligation, Article 474 (b), 475 (2) (b) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>230</td><td>1.3.2.6.3<br><strong>Own T2 instruments</strong><br>Articles 66 point (a), 476, 477(2) and 478 of CRR<br>When determining the amount of the holdings to be deducted, institutions shall take into account the provisions of Article 67 of CRR.<br>Given that the treatment of the “residual amount” differs depending upon the nature of the instrument (Article 477(2) of CRR), institutions shall break down the above-mentioned holdings according to “direct” and “indirect” own Tier 2 holdings.<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 66 (a) of CRR.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>231</td><td><strong>of which: Direct holdings</strong><br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount of direct holdings, including instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase by virtue of an existing or contingent contractual obligation, Articles 476 (b) and 477 (2) (a) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>232</td><td><strong>of which: Indirect holdings</strong><br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Total amount of indirect holdings, including instruments that an institution could be obliged to purchase by virtue of an existing or contingent contractual obligation, Articles 476 (b) and 477 (2) (b) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>240</td><td>1.3.2.7.<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings</strong><br>Given that the treatment of the “residual amount” differs depending whether the holding of Common Equity Tier 1, Additional Tier 1 or Tier 2 in the financial sector entity is to be considered being significant or not (Articles 472(9), 475 (3) and 477 (3) of CRR), institutions shall break down reciprocal cross holdings according to significant investments and non-significant investments.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>250</td><td>1.3.2.7.1<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in CET1 Capital</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (g), 469 (1), 472(9) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(g) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>260</td><td>1.3.2.7.1.1<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in CET1 Capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (g), 469 (1), 472(9) point (a) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Residual amount according to Article 469(1)(b) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>270</td><td>1.3.2.7.1.2<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in CET1 Capital of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (g), 469 (1), 472(9) point (b) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Residual amount according to Article 469(1)(b) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>280</td><td>1.3.2.7.2<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in AT1 Capital</strong><br>Articles 56 point (b), 474, 475(3) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 56 (b) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>290</td><td>1.3.2.7.2.1<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in AT1 Capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 56 point (b), 474, 475(3) point (a) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Residual amount according to Article 475(3) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>300</td><td>1.3.2.7.2.2<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in AT1 Capital of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 56 point (b), 474, 475(3) point (b) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Residual amount according to Article 475(3) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>310</td><td>1.3.2.7.3<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in T2 Capital</strong><br>Articles 66 point (b), 476, 477(3) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 66 (b) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>320</td><td>1.3.2.7.3.1<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in T2 Capital of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 66 point (b), 476, 477(3) point (a) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Residual amount according to Article 477(3) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>330</td><td>1.3.2.7.3.2<br><strong>Reciprocal cross holdings in T2 Capital of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 66 point (b), 476, 477(3) point (b) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Residual amount according to Article 477(3) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>340</td><td>1.3.2.8.<br><strong>Own funds instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong></td></tr>
> <tr><td>350</td><td>1.3.2.8.1<br><strong>CET1 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (h), 469 (1), 472(10) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(h) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>360</td><td>1.3.2.8.2<br><strong>AT1 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 56 point (c), 474, 475(4) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 56 (c) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>370</td><td>1.3.2.8.3<br><strong>T2 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution does not have a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 66 point (c), 476, 477(4) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 66 (c) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>380</td><td>1.3.2.9<br><strong>Deferred tax assets that are dependent on future profitability and arise from temporary differences and CET1 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Article 470(2) and (3) of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Article 470(1) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>390</td><td>1.3.2.10<br><strong>Own funds instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong></td></tr>
> <tr><td>400</td><td>1.3.2.10.1<br><strong>CET1 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 36(1) point (i), 469 (1), 472(11) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 36(1)(i) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>410</td><td>1.3.2.10.2<br><strong>AT1 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 56 point (d), 474, 475(4) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 56 (d) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>420</td><td>1.3.2.10.2<br><strong>T2 instruments of financial sector entities where the institution has a significant investment</strong><br>Articles 66 point (d), 476, 477(4) and 478 of CRR<br>The amount to be reported in column 060 of this row: Original deduction Article 66 (d) of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>425</td><td>1.3.2.11<br><strong>Exemption from deduction of Equity Holdings in Insurance Companies from CET 1 Items</strong><br>Article 471 of CRR</td></tr>
> <tr><td>430</td><td>1.3.3<br><strong>Additional filters and deductions</strong><br>Article 481 of CRR<br>This row reflects the overall effect of transitional provisions on additional filters and deductions.<br>In accordance with Article 481 of CRR, institutions shall report in item 1.3.3 information relating to the filters and deductions required under the national transposition measures for Articles 57 and 66 of Directive 2006/48/EC and for Articles 13 and 16 of Directive 2006/49/EC, and which are not required in accordance with Part Two.</td></tr>
> </tbody>
> </table>
>
> 1.6.3.
>
> C 05.02 — Grandfathered instruments: instruments not constituing state aid (CA5.2)
>
> 25\.
>
> Institutions shall report information in relation with the transitional provisions of grandfathered instruments not constituting state aid (Article 484 to 491 of CRR).
>
> 1.6.3.1.
>
> Instructions concerning specific positions
>
> <table>
> <tbody>
> <tr><th colspan="2">Columns</th></tr>
> <tr><td>010</td><td><strong>Amount of instruments plus related share premium</strong><br>Article 484(3) to (5) of CRR<br>Instruments which are eligible for each respective row, including their related share premiums.</td></tr>

> <tr><td>060</td><td><strong>Total grandfathered amount</strong><br>The amount to be reported shall be equal to the amounts reported in the respective columns in row 060 of CA 5.1.</td></tr>
> </tbody>
> </table>
>

> <tr><td>060</td><td>2.2.2<br><strong>Instruments with a call exercisable after the reporting date, and which do not meet the conditions in Article 49 of CRR after the date of effective maturity</strong><br>Articles 489(5), and 491 point (a) of CRR<br>The amount to be reported shall include the related share premium accounts.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>070</td><td>2.2.3<br><strong>Instruments with a call exercisable prior to or on 20 July 2011, and which do not meet the conditions in Article 49 of CRR after the date of effective maturity</strong><br>Articles 489(6) and 491 point (c) of CRR<br>The amount to be reported shall include the related share premium accounts</td></tr>
> <tr><td>080</td><td>2.3<br><strong>Excess on the limit of CET1 grandfathered instruments</strong><br>Article 487(1) of CRR<br>The excess on the limit of CET1 grandfathered instruments may be treated as instruments which can be grandfathered as AT1 instruments.</td></tr>

> <tr><td>110</td><td>3.2<br><strong>Grandfathered items with an incentive to redeem</strong><br>Article 490 of CRR</td></tr>

> <tr><td>130</td><td>3.2.2<br><strong>Items with a call exercisable after the reporting date, and which do not meet the conditions in Article 63 of CRR after the date of effective maturity</strong><br>Articles 490(5), and 491 point (a) of CRR<br>The amount to be reported shall include the related share premium accounts.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>140</td><td>3.2.3<br><strong>Items with a call exercisable prior to or on 20 July 2011, and which do not meet the conditions in Article 63 of CRR after the date of effective maturity</strong><br>Articles 490(6) and 491 point (c) of CRR<br>The amount to be reported shall include the related share premium accounts.</td></tr>
> <tr><td>150</td><td>3.3<br><strong>Excess on the limit of AT1 grandfathered instruments</strong><br>Article 487(2) of CRR<br>The excess on the limit of AT1 grandfathered instruments may be treated as instruments which can be grandfathered as T2 instruments.</td></tr>
